 Such a pity about the death of uncle Tiberius. Well, I guess I should show him proper respect; I will cover myself with ashes, let my beard get shaggy and my hair unkempt, (I’ll still be a handsome devil) and, of course, I’ll have to wear that drab, dirty toga. What was it called again?	ANSWER: TOGA PULLA BONUS: I’m over that now, time to gussy up for my upcoming triumph; I’ll be driven in that sporty triumphal chariot and, although I might be “red in the face,” I will look dashing, as usual, in my toga picta. Complete the following mythological analogy: Helios: Sol:: Selene: ________. ANSWER:LUNA BONUS: What lover of Selene was given eternal youth but had to sleep eternally? ANSWER:ENDYMION BONUS: What lover of Helios turned into a sunflower? ANSWER:CLYTIE

What king killed Theseus by pushing him over a cliff? ANSWER:LYCOMEDES BONUS: What bandit did Theseus kill in a similar way, although the bandit was eaten by a turtle? ANSWER:SCEIRON BONUS: What hero did Lycomedes later help hide when Agamemnon was drafting men for the Trojan War? ANSWER:ACHILLES 
Whose form did Athena take in order to trick Hector in his one-on-one battle with Achilles? ANSWER:DEIPHOBUS BONUS: What wife of Deiphobus betrayed him to Menelaus and Odysseus during the fall of Troy? ANSWER:HELEN BONUS: What brother of Deiphobus also vied for Helen’s hand after the death of Paris? ANSWER:HELENUS 
As referenced in an earlier question, Circe lived on the island of Aeaea. What brother of Circe lived with his daughter Medea in the similarly named city of Aea? ANSWER:AEETES BONUS: What sister of Aeetes married Minos and lived in Knossos on Crete? ANSWER:PASIPHAE BONUS: What god was the father of Aeetes, Circe, and Pasiphae? ANSWER:HELIUS 
Why was Ixion punished in the Underworld? ANSWER:HE TRIED TO SEDUCE HERA/JUNO BONUS: What was the name of the cloud woman whom Zeus placed in Ixion’s bed in order to test him? ANSWER:NEPHELE BONUS: Ixion’s children were as abominable as he was. What race of creatures were said to be the descendants of Ixion? ANSWER:CENTAURS 
What was the name of the North African king who gave Dido as much land as she could encompass in the hide of an ox? ANSWER:IARBAS BONUS: How did Dido encompass enough land to create the outline of a citadel? ANSWER:SHE CUT THE HIDE INTO VERY THIN STRIPS AND TIED THEM END-TO-END BONUS: What was the name of this citadel, which according to the Greeks was named after the ox hide? ANSWER:BYRSA 
What king of Troy broke his word with two gods and a hero? ANSWER:LAOMEDON BONUS: The two gods that Laomedon broke his word with were Apollo and Poseidon. What punishment did each god inflict on the hapless citizens of Troy? ANSWER:APOLLO SENT A PLAGUE, POSEIDON SENT A SEA MONSTER BONUS: Laomedon apparently did not learn from his mistakes and later broke a promise he made to Heracles. What did Heracles do to gain revenge? ANSWER:SACKED TROY (AND KILLED LAOMEDON) 
What Roman goddess of fruit trees rejected all suitors and shut herself in her orchard? ANSWER:POMONA BONUS: What suitor eventually convinced Pomona to marry him? ANSWER:VERTUMNUS BONUS: What disguise did Vertumnus adopt in order to gain Pomona’s trust and convince her to marry him? ANSWER:AN OLD WOMAN 
What follower of Artemis had a child by Zeus named Arcas? ANSWER:CALLISTO BONUS: What guise did Zeus adopt when he seduced Callisto? ANSWER:ARTEMIS BONUS: Who eventually killed Callisto? ANSWER:ARCAS OR ARTEMIS 
What bronze giant guarded the island of Crete until Medea killed him? ANSWER:TALUS BONUS: Talus was originally charged with guarding what lover of Zeus? ANSWER:EUROPA BONUS: What was the name of the hound that could catch anything which was also given as a present to Europa? ANSWER:LAELAPS 
What goddess did Hera send down to prevent the birth of Heracles? ANSWER:EILEITHYIA BONUS: What attendant of Alcmene tricked Eileithyia into allowing Alcmene to give birth? ANSWER:GALANTHIS BONUS: Into what did Eileithyia change Galanthis? ANSWER:A WEASEL 
What sea nymph was the mother of Polyphemus? ANSWER:THOOSA BONUS: Polyphemus fell in love with the sea nymph Galatea. However Galatea was in love with what young man? ANSWER:ACIS BONUS: Polyphemus found out about Acis and Galatea and killed Acis by throwing the top of a mountain on him. Into what was Acis transformed? ANSWER:A RIVER GOD 
What hero, by some accounts, was the son of Eurynome and Glaucus and the grandson of Sisyphus? ANSWER:BELLEROPHON/HIPPONOUS BONUS: Glaucus was renowned for his racing horses. What did Glaucus do to give them the competitive edge? ANSWER:FED THEM HUMAN FLESH BONUS: What god was normally said to be the father of Bellerophon? ANSWER:POSEIDON 
Into what was the Thracian king Tereus changed after he tried to kill his wife Procne and her sister Philomela? ANSWER:HOOPOE BONUS: Into what was Procne changed? ANSWER:NIGHTINGALE/SWALLOW BONUS: Into what was Philomela changed? ANSWER:SWALLOW/NIGHTINGALE 
Which of the Seven Against Thebes was the son of Atalanta? ANSWER:PARTHENOPAEUS BONUS: What god was sometimes said to be the father of Parthenopaeus? ANSWER:ARES BONUS: What son of Heracles and Auge, more famous for being wounded and then healed by Achilles, was exposed with Parthenopaeus and became his close friend? ANSWER:TELEPHUS 
What king of Pylos was father of Nestor? ANSWER:NELEUS BONUS: What brother of Nestor was able to change shape and sailed with the Argonauts? ANSWER:PERICLYMENUS BONUS: What daughter of Amphion and Niobe was the mother of Nestor? ANSWER:CHLORIS 
Besides Pegasus, what other being sprang from the neck of Medusa after she was killed by Perseus? ANSWER:CHRYSAOR BONUS: What Oceanid did Chrysaor sire at least one son with? ANSWER:CALLIRRHOE BONUS: Who was this monstrous son, most famous for having a herd of cattle guarded by Eurytion? ANSWER:GERYON 
Who taught the riddle to the sphinx? ANSWER:THE MUSES BONUS: What Muse was the mother of the Thracian king, Rhesus? ANSWER:CALLIOPE/EUTERPE BONUS: What Muse was the mother of Apollo’s lover, Hyacinthus? ANSWER:CLEIO
